k_{0}=z_{\beta}
when using constant stepsize.

A.1  Notation

We begin with a summary of some notation that is used in the proof.

1.

Given a pair of matrices
X_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|\times|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
,
X_{-i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|\times|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
and a pair of distributions
\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|},\mu^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
, we define

\displaystyle V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})=\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\hat{\mu}^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i}-\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})-\tau\nu(\mu^{i})\right\},

(27)

where
\nu(\mu^{i})=-\sum_{a^{i}}\mu^{i}(a^{i})\log(\mu^{i}(a^{i}))
is the entropy function.

2.

Given a pair of
v
-functions
(v^{i},v^{-i})
and a state
s\in\mathcal{S}
, when
X_{i}=\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)
and
X_{-i}=\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)
, we write
V_{v,s}(\cdot,\cdot)
for
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
.

3.

For any
(\pi^{i},\pi^{-i})
and
s
, define
v^{i}_{*,\pi^{-i}}(s)=\max_{\hat{\pi}^{i}}v^{i}_{\hat{\pi}^{i},\pi^{-i}}(s)
,
v^{i}_{\pi^{i},*}=\min_{\hat{\pi}^{-i}}v^{i}_{\pi^{i},\hat{\pi}^{-i}}(s)
,
v^{-i}_{\pi^{-i},*}(s)=\min_{\hat{\pi}^{i}}v^{-i}_{\pi^{-i},\hat{\pi}^{i}}(s)
, and
v^{-i}_{*,\pi^{i}}(s)=\max_{\hat{\pi}^{-i}}v^{-i}_{\hat{\pi}^{-i},\hat{\pi}^{i}}(s)
. Note that we have
v^{i}_{*,\pi^{-i}}+v^{-i}_{\pi^{-i},*}=0
and
v^{i}_{\pi^{i},*}+v^{-i}_{*,\pi^{i}}=0
because of the zero-sum structure.

4.

Denote
v_{*}^{i}
(respectively,
v_{*}^{-i}
) as the unique fixed-point of the equation
\mathcal{B}^{i}(v^{i})=v^{i}
(respectively,
\mathcal{B}^{-i}(v^{-i})=v^{-i}
). Note that we have
v_{*}^{i}+v_{*}^{-i}=0
.

A.2  Boundedness of the Iterates

We first show in the following two lemmas that all the
q
-functions and
v
-functions generated by Algorithm
2
are uniformly bounded from above, and the policies are uniformly bounded from below.

Lemma A.1

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.1
)
.

It holds for all
t,k\geq 0
and
i\in\{1,2\}
that

1.

\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,

2.

\|q_{t,k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

Lemma A.2

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.2
)
.

It holds for all
t,k\geq 0
and
(s,a^{i},a^{-i})
that

1.

\pi_{t,k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
,

2.

\pi_{t,k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
,

where
\ell_{\tau}=[1+(A_{\max}-1)\exp(2/[(1-\gamma)\tau])]^{-1}
.

A.3  Analysis of the Outer-Loop:
v
-Function Update

Our ultimate goal is to bound the expected Nash gap

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\textit{NG}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\pi^{i}}U^{i}(\pi^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})-U^{i}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})\right)\right].

We first bound the Nash gap using the value functions of the output policies of Algorithm
2
.

Lemma A.3

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.4
)
.

The following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\pi^{i}}U^{i}(\pi^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})-U^{i}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})\right)\leq\sum_{i=1,2}\left\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}\right\|_{\infty}.

(28)

The next lemmas connects the RHS of Eq. (
28
) to the
v
-function iterates
\{(v_{t}^{i},v_{t}^{-i})\}_{t\geq 0}
of Algorithm
2
.

Lemma A.4

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.5
)
.

It holds for all
t\geq 0
and
i=1,2
that

\displaystyle\left\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\bigg(2\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})\bigg).

In view of Lemma
A.4
, we need to further bound the terms
\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}
,
\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}
, and

\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))
. We first consider
\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}
, and establish a one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for it.

Lemma A.5

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.6
)
.

It holds for all
t\geq 0
and
i=1,2
that

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}+2\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

(29)

Our next step is to control
\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}
. Similar to
\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}
, we also establish a one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for
\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}
in the following lemma.

Lemma A.6

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.7
)
.

It holds for all
t\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}+v_{t+1}^{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}.

In view of Lemma
A.5
and Lemma
A.6
, our next task is to control the following two terms:
\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}
, and
\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))
. For ease of exposition, we write down only the inner-loop of Algorithm
2
in the following. All results derived for the
q
-functions and policies of Algorithm
3
can be directly combined with the outer-loop of Algorithm
2
using a simple conditioning argument together with the Markov property.

Algorithm 3

Inner-Loop of Algorithm
2

1:

Input:
Integer
K
, initializations
q_{0}^{i}=\bm{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
and
\pi_{0}^{i}(a^{i}|s)=1/|\mathcal{A}^{i}|
for all
s\in\mathcal{S}
, and a joint
v
-function
v=(v^{i},v^{-i})
from the outer-loop satisfying
\max(\|v^{-i}\|_{\infty},\|v^{i}\|_{\infty})\leq 1/(1-\gamma)
.

2:

for

k=0,1,\cdots,K-1

do

3:

\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s)=\pi_{k}^{i}(s)+\beta_{k}(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{i}(s))
for all
s\in\mathcal{S}

4:

Sample

A_{k}^{i}\sim\pi_{k+1}^{i}(\cdot\mid S_{k})
, and observe
S_{k+1}\sim p(\cdot\mid S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i})

5:

q_{k+1}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})=q_{k}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})+\alpha_{k}\left(\mathcal{R}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i})+\gamma v^{i}(S_{k+1})-q_{k}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\right)

6:

end

for

7:

Output:

q_{K}^{i}
and
\pi_{K}^{i}

A.4  Analysis of the Inner-Loop: Policy Update

We consider
\{(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})\}_{k\geq 0}
generated by Algorithm
3
, and use
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
defined in Eq. (
27
) as the Lyapunov function to study them. For simplicity of notation, we use
\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
(respectively,
\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
) to denote the gradient with respect to the first argument (respectively, the second argument). The following lemma establishes the strongly convexity and the smoothness of
V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
. We only state the results regarding the argument
\mu^{i}
. Similar results also hold for the argument
\mu^{-i}
.

Lemma A.7

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.8
)
.

The function
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
has the following properties.

1.

For any
\mu^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
,
V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
as a function of
\mu^{i}
is
\tau
– strongly convex with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
.

2.

For any
\delta_{i}>0
and
\mu^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
,
V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
as a function of
\mu^{i}
is
L_{\tau}
– smooth on
\{\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\mid\min_{a^{i}}\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\delta_{i}\}
with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
, where
L_{\tau}=\frac{\sigma^{2}_{\max}(X_{-i})}{\tau}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}
.

3.

It holds for any
(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{7}{8}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})+\frac{16}{\tau}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}.

4.

For any
u^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|},u^{-i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
, we have for all
(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})\in\{\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|},\mu^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}\mid\min_{a^{i}}\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\delta_{i},\min_{a^{-i}}\mu^{-i}(a^{-i})\geq\delta_{-i}\}
(where
\delta_{i},\delta_{-i}>0
) that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle+\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}+\|X_{i}\|_{2}+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\right)\bigg[\frac{2\bar{c}}{\tau}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|u^{i}-X_{i}\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|u^{-i}-X_{i}\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

where
\bar{c}
is any positive real number.

With the properties of
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
established above, we can now use it as a Lyapunov function to study
\pi_{k}^{i}
and
\pi_{k}^{-i}
. Specifically, using the smoothness of
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
, the update equation in Algorithm
3
Line
3
, and Lemma
A.7
(3) and (4), we have the desired one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for
\sum_{s}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))
, which is presented in the following.

Lemma A.8

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.9
)
.

The following inequality holds for all
k\geq 0
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s))]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}}{4}\right)\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))]+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{i=1,2}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

A.5  Analysis of the Inner-Loop:
q
-Function Update

In this section, we consider
q_{k}^{i}
generated by Algorithm
3
.
We begin by reformulating the update of the
q
-function as a stochastic approximation algorithm for estimating a time-varying target. Let
F^{i}:\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}^{i}\times\mathcal{A}^{-i}\times\mathcal{S}\mapsto\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
be an operator defined as

\displaystyle[F^{i}(q^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})](s,a^{i})=\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})\}}\left(\mathcal{R}^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i})+\gamma v^{i}(s_{1})-q^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})\right)

for all
(q^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})
and
(s,a^{i})
. Then Algorithm
3
Line
5
can be compactly written as

\displaystyle q_{k+1}^{i}=q_{k}^{i}+\alpha_{k}F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1}).

(30)

Denote the stationary distribution of the Markov chain
\{S_{k}\}
induced by the joint policy
\pi_{k}=(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})
by
\mu_{k}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{S}|}
, the existence and uniqueness of which is guaranteed by Lemma
A.2
and Lemma
4.1
(1).
Let
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}:\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\mapsto\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
be defined as

\displaystyle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})=\mathbb{E}_{S_{0}\sim\mu_{k}(\cdot),A_{0}^{i}\sim\pi_{k}^{i}(\cdot|S_{0}),A_{k}^{-i}\sim\pi_{k}^{-i}(\cdot|S_{0}),S_{1}\sim p(\cdot|S_{0},A_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{-i})}\left[F^{i}(q^{i},S_{0},A_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{-i},S_{1})\right]

for all
q^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
.
Then Eq. (
30
) can be viewed as a stochastic approximation algorithm for solving the (time-varying) equation
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})=0
with time-inhomogeneous Markovian noise
\{(S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\}_{k\geq 0}
. We next establish the properties of the operators
F^{i}(\cdot)
and
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(\cdot)
in the following lemma.

Lemma A.9

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.10
)
.

The following inequalities hold:

1.

\|F^{i}(q_{1}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})-F^{i}(q_{2}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})\|_{2}\leq\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}
for any
(q_{1}^{i},q_{2}^{i})
and
(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})
.

2.

\|F^{i}(\bm{0},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})
.

3.

\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})=0
has a unique solution
\bar{q}_{k}^{i}
, which is explicitly given as
\bar{q}_{k}^{i}(s)=\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)
for all
s
.

4.

\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{1}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{2}^{i}),q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\rangle\leq-c_{\tau}\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}
for all
(q_{1}^{i},q_{2}^{i})
.

Using
\|\cdot\|_{2}^{2}
as a Lyapunov function and we have by the equivalent update equation (
30
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}+q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}+\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]+\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{1}}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]+\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle].

(31)

What remains to do is to bound the terms on the RHS of the previous inequality. Among them, we want to highlight the two terms
N_{1}
and
N_{2}
. For the term
N_{1}
, using Lemma
A.9
(4) and we have

\displaystyle N_{1}=\mathbb{E}[\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]=\mathbb{E}[\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(\bar{q}_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]\leq-c_{\tau}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}],

(32)

which provides us the desired negative drift.

The term
N_{2}
involves the difference between the operator
F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})
and its expected version
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})
, and hence can be viewed as the stochastic error due to sampling. The fact that the Markov chain
\{(S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\}
is time-inhomogeneous presents major theoretical challenges in our analysis. To overcome this challenge, observe that: (1) the policy (hence the transition probability matrix of the induced Markov chain) is changing slowly compared to the
q
-function; see Algorithm
3
Line
3
, and (2) the stationary distribution as a function of the policy is Lipschitz (cf. Lemma
4.1
(3)). These two observations together enable us to develop a refined conditioning argument to handle the time-inhomogeneous Markovian noise. The result is presented in following. Similar ideas were previous used in
Bhandari et al., 2018
;
Srikant and Ying, 2019
;
Chen et al., 2021b
;
Zou et al., 2019
;
Khodadadian et al., 2022
for finite-sample analysis of single-agent RL algorithms.

Lemma A.10

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.11
)
.

When
\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}\leq 1/4
for all
k\geq z_{k}
, we have for all
k\geq z_{k}
that

\displaystyle N_{2}\leq\frac{340|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

When using constant stepsize, we have
z_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}=z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha=\mathcal{O}(\alpha\log^{2}(1/\beta))
. Since the two stepsizes
\alpha
and
\beta
differ only by a multiplicative constant
c_{\alpha,\beta}
, we have
\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 0}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha=0
. Similarly, we also have
\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}z_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}=0
when using diminishing stepsizes. Therefore, Lemma
A.10
implies
N_{2}=o(1)
.

We next bound the rest of terms on the RHS of Eq. (
31
) in the following lemma.

Lemma A.11

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.12
)
.

The following inequalities
hold for all
k\geq 0
.

1.

\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

2.

\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

3.

\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]\leq\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

4.

\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]\leq\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))]
.

Using the upper bounds we obtained for all the terms on the RHS of Eq. (
31
) and we have the one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for
q_{k}^{i}
. Following the same line of analysis and we also obtain the one-step inequality for
q_{k}^{-i}
. Both results are presented in the following lemma.

Lemma A.12

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.13
)
.

The following inequality holds for all
k\geq z_{k}
and
i\in\{1,2\}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))].

A.6  Solving Coupled Lyapunov Drift Inequalities

We first restate the Lyapunov drift inequalities from previous sections. For simplicity of notation, we denote
\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)=\sum_{i=1,2}\|q_{t,k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}
,
\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)=\sum_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,k}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,k}^{-i}(s))
, and
\mathcal{F}_{t}
as the history of Algorithm
2
right before the
t
-th outer-loop iteration. Note that
v_{t}^{i}
and
v_{t}^{-i}
are both measurable with respect to
\mathcal{F}_{t}
. In what follows, we denote
\mathbb{E}_{t}[\;\cdot\;]
for
\mathbb{E}[\;\cdot\;\mid\mathcal{F}_{t}]
.

•

Lemma
A.5
:
It holds for all
t\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}+2\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+2\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,K)+\sum_{i=1,2}\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}.

(33)

•

Lemma
A.6
:
It holds for all
t\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}+v_{t+1}^{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\sum_{i=1,2}\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}.

(34)

•

Lemma
A.8
:
It holds for all
t,k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k+1)]\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}}{4}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle+\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(35)

•

Lemma
A.12
:
It holds for all
t\geq 0
and
k\geq z_{k}
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k+1)]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

(36)

Adding up Eqs. (
•
‣
A.6
) and (
•
‣
A.6
) and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k+1)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k+1)]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{k}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]+\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]+\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

Note that Condition
A.1
implies that

\displaystyle\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\leq\frac{c_{\tau}}{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k+1)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k+1)]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]+\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

(37)

A.6.1
Constant Stepsize

When using constant stepsizes, i.e.,
\alpha_{k}\equiv\alpha
,
\beta_{k}\equiv\beta
, and
\beta=c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha
, repeatedly using Eq. (
A.6
) from
z_{\beta}
to
k
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}(\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,0)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,0))

\displaystyle+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{704|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha.

(38)

We next bound
\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,0)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,0)
.
For
i\in\{1,2\}
, since
\pi_{t,0}^{i}
is initialized at a uniformly random policy and
q_{t,0}^{i}=\bm{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,0)=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,0}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,0}^{-i}(s))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}\{(\mu^{i}-\pi_{t,0}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,0}^{-i}(s)+\tau\nu(\mu^{i})-\tau\nu(\pi_{t,0}^{i}(s))\}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\sum_{s}\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{s,a^{i},a^{-i}}|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|}{(1-\gamma)},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,0)=\sum_{i=1,2}\|\bar{q}_{t,0}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{2|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Using the previous two bounds in Eq. (
38
) and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{704|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha,

(39)

which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{704|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{712|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha.

Substituting the previous inequality on
\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]
into Eq. (
•
‣
A.6
) and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k+1)]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{16}\left(\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\frac{712|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\tau}\alpha}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{4(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{45|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{2},

where the last line follows from Condition
A.1
. Repeatedly using the previous inequality from
z_{\beta}
to
k
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\tau}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha(k-z_{\beta})}{4(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k-z_{\beta}-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{90|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{c_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha.

The next step is to substitute the previous bound on
\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]
into Eq. (
34
). To achieve that, first note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}\right]\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\left(\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

(
\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}\leq 2\sqrt{a+b}
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}_{t}^{1/2}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)].

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{3\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\tau}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}}{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha^{1/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}\tau^{1/2}}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\frac{20|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2}.

(40)

Taking the total expectation on both sides of the previous inequality then using the result in Eq. (
34
), and we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t+1}^{i}+v_{t+1}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\gamma+\frac{4\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}\tau^{1/2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]

\displaystyle+\frac{4\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{20|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]

\displaystyle+\frac{4\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{20|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2},

where the last line follows from Condition
A.1
. Since
\|v_{0}^{i}+v_{0}^{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{1-\gamma}
, repeatedly using the previous inequality starting from
0
and we have for all
t\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{t}+\frac{8\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{40|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2}.

(41)

Now we have obtained finite-sample bounds for
\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)
,
\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)
, and
\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}
. The next step is to use them in Eq. (
•
‣
A.6
) to obtain finite-sample bounds for
\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}
. Specifically, we have by Eq. (
•
‣
A.6
), Eq. (
A.6.1
), and Eq. (
A.6.1
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,K)]+\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,K)]+2\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{K-z_{\beta}}+\frac{64|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{1424|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha+\frac{3\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\tau}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha^{1/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}\tau^{1/2}}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+\frac{20|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+\frac{134|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\frac{12|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1444|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})+\frac{1444|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{12|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{134|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)}\left(\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{t}+\frac{40|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}^{3/4}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{1/2}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{\beta}\alpha^{1/2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{8\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})+\frac{268|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{1084|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{6804|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{3}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2}.

Repeatedly using the previous inequality from
0
to
T-1
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{T}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4\tau\log(A_{\max})}{1-\gamma}+\frac{270|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{T-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1084|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{6804|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{4}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2},

where we used
\|v_{0}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 2/(1-\gamma)
.

Our next step is to use the bounds we obtained for
\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)
,
\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)
,
\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}
, and
\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}
in Lemma
A.4
. For simplicity, we use
a\lesssim b
to mean that there exists a
numerical
constant
c
such that
a\leq cb
. Now, we have by the previous inequality, Eq. (
A.6.1
), and Eq. (
A.6.1
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}\right\|_{\infty}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\left(2\mathbb{E}[\|v_{T}^{i}+v_{T}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|v^{i}_{T}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}]+\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(T,K)+2\tau\log(A_{\max})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\log(A_{\max})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{T-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{5}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{5}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2},

Finally, using the previous inequality in Lemma
A.3
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\textit{NG}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\log(A_{\max})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2}\right)^{T-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}(K-z_{\beta})^{1/2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{5}}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{K-z_{\beta}-1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{5}}z_{\beta}^{2}\alpha^{1/2}.

The proof of Theorem
3.1
is complete.

A.6.2
Diminishing Stepsizes

Consider using linearly diminishing stepsizes, i.e.,
\alpha_{k}=\frac{\alpha}{k+h}
,
\beta_{k}=\frac{\beta}{k+h}
, and
\beta=c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha
. Repeatedly using Eq. (
A.6
) and we have for all
k\geq k_{0}
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\underbrace{\prod_{m=k_{0}}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)}_{\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\underbrace{\sum_{n=k_{0}}^{k-1}z_{n}^{2}\alpha_{n}^{2}\prod_{m=n+1}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)}_{\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{n=k_{0}}^{k-1}\alpha_{n}\prod_{m=n+1}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)}_{\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{3}}.

We next provide estimates for the terms
\{\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 3}
. Bounds of terms like
\{\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 3}
are well-established in existing work studying the convergence rate of iterative algorithms
(
Srikant and Ying, 2019
;
Lan, 2020
;
Chen et al., 2021b
)
. Specifically, we have from
(
Chen et al., 2021b
, Appendix A.2.)
that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{1}\leq\left(\frac{k_{0}+h}{k+h}\right)^{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2},\quad\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{2}\leq\frac{4ez_{k}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2-1}\frac{1}{k+h},\;\text{ and }\;\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{3}\leq\frac{2}{c_{\alpha,\beta}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)+\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{k_{0}+h}{k+h}\right)^{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{z_{k}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2-1}\frac{1}{k+h}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}}\right)^{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{z_{k}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2-1}\frac{1}{k+h}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2},

(42)

which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}}\right)^{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{z_{k}^{2}\alpha^{2}}{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha/2-1}\frac{1}{k+h}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

Using the previous bound on
\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]
in Eq. (
•
‣
A.6
) and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k+1)]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,k)]+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\tau}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

Repeatedly using the previous inequality starting from
k_{0}
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,k)]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}}\right)^{c_{\tau}\alpha/2}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}^{2}\alpha_{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{2}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}^{2}\alpha_{k}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}c_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}\tau}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

Since
\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}\right]\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{t}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)]^{1/2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|q_{t,K}^{i}-\bar{q}_{t,K}^{i}\|_{2}\right]\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}^{\prime}|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}}{\alpha^{1/2}_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)}z_{k}\alpha_{k}^{1/2}+\frac{c_{2}^{\prime}\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}\tau^{1/2}}\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty},

(43)

where
c_{1}^{\prime}
and
c_{2}^{\prime}
are numerical constants.
Taking total expectation on boths sides of the previous inequality and then using the result in Eq. (
34
), and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t+1}^{i}+v_{t+1}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\gamma+\frac{c_{2}^{\prime}\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}c^{1/2}_{\alpha,\beta}}{c_{\tau}^{1/2}\tau^{1/2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+\frac{c_{1}^{\prime}|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}}{\alpha^{1/2}_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)}z_{k}\alpha_{k}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+\frac{c_{1}^{\prime}|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}}{\alpha^{1/2}_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)}z_{k}\alpha_{k}^{1/2},

where the last line follows from Condition
A.1
. Repeatedly using the previous inequality starting from
0
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]\lesssim\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{t}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{3/4}A_{\max}}{\alpha^{1/2}_{k_{0}}(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}^{1/2}.

(44)

The next step is to bound
\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}
. Recall from Eq. (
•
‣
A.6
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,K)]+2\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)]^{1/2}.

Since Eq. (
A.6.2
) and Eq. (
43
) imply that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}]+\tau\log(A_{\max})+\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{\pi}(t,K)]+\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}_{q}(t,K)]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{t}+\tau\log(A_{\max})+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{3}}z_{K}^{2}\alpha_{K}^{1/2},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\mathbb{E}[\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]

\displaystyle+c^{\prime\prime}\left[\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{t}+\tau\log(A_{\max})+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{3}}z_{K}^{2}\alpha_{K}^{1/2}\right]

for some numerical constant
c^{\prime\prime}
. Repeatedly using the previous inequality starting from
0
to
T-1
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{T}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}]\lesssim\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{T-1}+\frac{\tau\log(A_{\max})}{(1-\gamma)}+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{4}}z_{K}^{2}\alpha_{K}^{1/2}

Using the previous inequality, Eq. (
A.6.2
), and Eq. (
44
)
in Lemma
A.4
, and we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}\|_{\infty}]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{T-1}+\frac{\tau\log(A_{\max})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{5}}z_{K}^{2}\alpha_{K}^{1/2}

Finally, we have by the previous inequality and Lemma
A.3
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\textit{NG}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})]\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}T}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{2}\right)^{T-1}+\frac{\tau\log(A_{\max})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathcal{S}|^{2}A_{\max}^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{\alpha_{k_{0}}c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-\gamma)^{5}}z_{K}^{2}\alpha_{K}^{1/2}.

The proof of Theorem
3.2
is complete.

A.7  Proof of All Supporting Lemmas

A.7.1
Proof of Lemma
A.1

We first show by induction that whenever
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
, we have
\|q_{t,k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
k\geq 0
. Note that
\|q_{t,0}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
holds by our initialization. Suppose that
\|q_{t,k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for some
k\geq 0
. Then we have for all
(s,a^{i})
that

\displaystyle|q_{t,k+1}^{i}(s,a^{i})|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle|q_{t,k}^{i}(s,a^{i})+\alpha_{k}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\}}(\mathcal{R}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i})+\gamma v_{t}^{i}(S_{k+1})-q_{t,k}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i}))|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{k}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\}})|q_{t,k}^{i}(s,a^{i})|

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\}}|\mathcal{R}^{i}(S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i})+\gamma v_{t}^{i}(S_{k+1})|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{k}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\}})\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\alpha_{k}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(S_{k},A_{k}^{i})\}}\left(1+\frac{\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)

(45)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma},

where Eq. (
45
) follows from the induction hypothesis
\|q_{t,0}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
, and
\max_{s,a^{i},a^{-i}}|\mathcal{R}^{i}(s,a^{i},a^{-i})|\leq 1
. The induction is now complete and we have
\|q_{t,k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
k\geq 0
whenever
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

We next again use induction to show that
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
t\geq 0
. Our initialization ensures that
\|v_{0}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
. Suppose that
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for some
t\geq 0
. Using the update equation for
v_{t+1}^{i}
(i.e., Algorithm
2
Line
8
) and the fact that
\|q_{t,k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
k\geq 0
, we have for all
s\in\mathcal{S}
that

\displaystyle|v_{t+1}^{i}(s)|=\left|\sum_{a^{i}\in\mathcal{A}^{i}}\pi^{i}_{t,K}(a^{i}|s)q_{t,K}^{i}(s,a^{i})\right|\leq\sum_{a^{i}\in\mathcal{A}^{i}}\pi^{i}_{t,K}(a^{i}|s)\|q_{t,K}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}.

The induction for
\{v_{t}^{i}\}
is now complete and we have
\|v_{t}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
for all
t\geq 0
.

A.7.2
Proof of Lemma
A.2

Observe that for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
j\in\{1,2,\cdots,d\}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(x_{j})}{\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\exp(x_{\ell})}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{\exp(x_{j})}{\exp(x_{j})+\sum_{\ell\neq j}\exp(x_{\ell})}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\sum_{\ell\neq j}\exp(x_{\ell}-x_{j})}

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+(d-1)\exp(2\|x\|_{\infty})}.

Therefore, since
\|q^{i}_{t,k}\|_{\infty}\leq 1/(1-\gamma)
for all
t,k\geq 0
(cf. Lemma
A.1
), we have for all
t,k\geq 0
and
(s,a^{i})
that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\tau}(q_{t,k}^{i}(s))(a^{i})\geq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+(A_{\max}-1)\exp(2/[(1-\gamma)\tau])}=\ell_{\tau}.

(46)

We next use induction to show that
\pi_{t,k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
for all
t,k\geq 0
. Given any
t\geq 0
, our uniform initialization of
\pi_{t,0}^{i}
ensures that
\pi_{t,0}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
for all
(s,a^{i})
. Now suppose that
\pi_{t,k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
for all
(s,a^{i})
for some
k\geq 0
. Then we have from Algorithm
2
Line
4
that

\displaystyle\pi_{t,k+1}^{i}(a^{i}|s)=\;

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{k})\pi_{t,k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)+\beta_{k}\sigma_{\tau}(q_{t,k}^{i}(s))(a^{i})

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{k})\ell_{\tau}+\beta_{k}\ell_{\tau}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\ell_{\tau},

where the inequality follows from Eq. (
46
) and the induction hypothesis. The induction is now complete and we have
\pi_{t,k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
for all
t,k\geq 0
and
(s,a^{i})
. Similarly, we also have
\pi_{t,k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
for all
t,k\geq 0
and
(s,a^{-i})
.

A.7.3
Proof of Lemma
4.1

Lemma
4.1
(1), (3), and (4) are identical to
(
Zhang et al., 2022c
, Proposition 3)
. We here only prove Lemma
4.1
(2).

Consider the Markov chain
\{S_{k}\}
induced by
\pi_{b}
.
Since
\{S_{k}\}
is irreducible and aperiodic, there exists a positive integer
r_{b}
such that
P_{\pi_{b}}^{r_{b}}
has strictly positive entries
(
Levin and Peres, 2017
, Proposition 1.7)
. Therefore, there exists
\delta_{b}\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle P_{\pi_{b}}^{r_{b}}(s,s^{\prime})\geq\delta_{b}\mu_{b}(s^{\prime})

for all
(s,s^{\prime})
. In addition, the constant
\rho_{b}
introduced after Assumption
3.1
is explicitly given as
\rho_{b}=\exp(-\delta_{b}/r_{b})
.
The previous two equations are from the proof of the Markov chain convergence theorem presented in
(
Levin and Peres, 2017
, Section 4.3)
.

Next we consider the Markov chain
\{S_{k}\}
induced by an arbitrary
\pi\in\Pi_{\delta}
. Since

\displaystyle\frac{\pi_{b}(a|s)}{\pi(a|s)}=\frac{\pi_{b}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\pi_{b}^{-i}(a^{i}|s)}{\pi^{i}(a^{i}|s)\pi^{-i}(a^{i}|s)}\leq\frac{1}{\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}},\quad\forall\;a=(a^{i},a^{-i})\text{ and }s,

we have for any
s,s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
and
k\geq 1
that

\displaystyle P_{\pi_{b}}^{k}(s,s^{\prime})=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{0}}P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}(s,s_{0})P_{\pi_{b}}(s_{0},s^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{0}}P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}(s,s_{0})\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\pi_{b}(a|s_{0})P_{a}(s_{0},s^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{0}}P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}(s,s_{0})\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\frac{\pi_{b}(a|s_{0})}{\pi(a|s_{0})}\pi(a|s_{0})P_{a}(s_{0},s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}}\sum_{s_{0}}P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}(s,s_{0})\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\pi(a|s_{0})P_{a}(s_{0},s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}}\sum_{s_{0}}P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}(s,s_{0})P_{\pi}(s_{0},s^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}}[P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}P_{\pi}](s,s^{\prime}).

Since the previous inequality holds for all
s
and
s^{\prime}
, we in fact have
\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}P_{\pi_{b}}^{k}\leq P^{k-1}_{\pi_{b}}P_{\pi}
(which is an entry-wise inequality). Repeatedly using the previous inequality and we obtain

\displaystyle(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{k}P_{\pi_{b}}^{k}\leq P_{\pi}^{k},

which implies

\displaystyle P_{\pi}^{r_{b}}(s,s^{\prime})\geq\;

\displaystyle(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}P_{\pi_{b}}^{r_{b}}(s,s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\frac{\mu_{b}(s^{\prime})}{\mu_{\pi}(s^{\prime})}\mu_{\pi}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}\mu_{\pi}(s^{\prime}).

Following the proof of the Markov chain convergence theorem in
(
Levin and Peres, 2017
, Section 4.3)
and we have

\displaystyle\|P_{\pi}^{k}(s,\cdot)-\mu_{\pi}(\cdot)\|_{\text{TV}}\leq\;

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min})^{k/r_{b}-1},\quad\forall\;s\in\mathcal{S},\;\pi\in\Pi_{\delta}.

(47)

Since
A_{\max}\geq 2
(otherwise there is no decision to make in this Markov game), we have
\delta_{i}\delta_{-i}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. It follows that
1-\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}>1/2
.
Using the previous inequality in Eq. (
47
) and we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\pi\in\Pi_{\delta}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|P_{\pi}^{k}(s,\cdot)-\mu_{\pi}(\cdot)\|_{\text{TV}}\leq\;

\displaystyle 2(1-\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min})^{k/r_{b}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\delta_{b}(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}k/r_{b}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2\rho_{b}^{(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}k}

(Recall that
\rho_{b}=\exp(-\delta_{b}/r_{b})
)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2\rho_{\delta}^{k}.

We next compute the mixing time. Using the previous inequality and the definition of the total variation distance, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\pi\in\Pi_{\delta}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|P_{\pi}^{k}(s,\cdot)-\mu_{\pi}(\cdot)\|_{\text{TV}}\leq\eta

as long as

\displaystyle k\geq\frac{\log(2/\eta)}{\log(1/\rho_{\delta})}=\frac{1}{(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}}\frac{\log(2/\eta)}{\log(1/\rho_{b})}\geq\frac{t_{\pi_{b},\eta}}{(\delta_{i}\delta_{-i})^{r_{b}}\mu_{b,\min}}.

A.7.4
Proof of Lemma
A.3

Using the definition of utility functions and we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\pi^{i}}U^{i}(\pi^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})-U^{i}(\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i})\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\pi^{i}}\mathbb{E}_{S\sim p_{o}}\left[v^{i}_{\pi^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\mathbb{E}_{S\sim p_{o}}\left[\max_{\pi^{i}}v^{i}_{\pi^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)\right]\right)

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\mathbb{E}_{S\sim p_{o}}\left[v^{i}_{*,\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}(S)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{T,K}^{i},\pi_{T,K}^{-i}}\right\|_{\infty}.

A.7.5
Proof of Lemma
A.4

For any
t\geq 0
,
s\in\mathcal{S}
, and
i\in\{1,2\}
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)\right|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},*}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}(s)-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},*}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle v^{i}_{*}(s)-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}(s)+v^{-i}_{*}(s)-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},*}(s)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|v^{-i}_{*}-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}\|_{\infty}+\|v^{i}_{*}-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},*}\|_{\infty}.

(48)

It remains to bound the two terms on the RHS of the previous inequality. For the first term, note that for any
s\in\mathcal{S}
and
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\;

\displaystyle v^{-i}_{*}(s)-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}(s)-v^{i}_{*}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)\mu^{-i}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)|

\displaystyle+|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)\mu^{-i}|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{\mu^{i}}|(\mu^{i})^{\top}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}})(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s))\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)|

\displaystyle+|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\mu^{-i}|

\displaystyle+|\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\mu^{-i}-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)\mu^{-i}|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\max_{\mu^{i}}|(\mu^{i})^{\top}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}})(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s))\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)|}_{\hat{E}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)|}_{\hat{E}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\mu^{-i}}_{\hat{E}_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{|\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\mu^{-i}-\max_{\mu^{i}}\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)\mu^{-i}|}_{\hat{E}_{4}}.

(49)

We next bound the terms
\{\hat{E}_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 4}
. For any
v_{1}^{i},v_{2}^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}|}
, we have for any
(s,a^{i},a^{-i})
that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{1}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{2}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\gamma|\mathbb{E}[v_{1}^{i}(S_{1})-v_{2}^{i}(S_{1})\mid S_{0}=s,A_{0}^{i}=a^{i},A_{0}^{-i}=a^{-i}]|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{1}^{i}-v_{2}^{i}\|_{\infty},

which implies
\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{1}^{i})-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{2}^{i})\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v_{1}^{i}-v_{2}^{i}\|_{\infty}
.
As a result, we have

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}})-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{4}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{*})\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}.

Bounding the term
\hat{E}_{3}
requires more effort. First observe that

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{3}\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\min_{\mu^{-i}}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\mu^{-i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\max_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{-i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)+\min_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{-i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)^{\top}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\max_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{-i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\max_{\mu^{-i}}(\mu^{-i})^{\top}[-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)]^{\top}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{\mu^{-i}}\left|(\mu^{-i})^{\top}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s)+[\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)]^{\top})\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{a^{i},a^{-i}}\left|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|,

where the last line follows from

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\gamma|\mathbb{E}[v_{t}^{i}(S_{1})+v_{t}^{i}(S_{1})\mid S_{0}=s,A_{0}^{i}=a^{i},A_{0}^{-i}=a^{-i}]|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\left\{\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right\}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left\{\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{\mu^{i}}\left\{(\mu^{i}-\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)+\tau\nu(\mu^{i})-\tau\nu(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))\right\}+2\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\max_{a^{i},a^{-i}}\left|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})+\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{3}\leq 2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max}).

(50)

Substituting the upper bounds we obtained for the terms
\{E_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 4}
into Eq. (
49
) and we have

\displaystyle\|v^{-i}_{*}-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v^{-i}_{*}-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max}),

which after rearranging terms implies

\displaystyle\|v^{-i}_{*}-v^{-i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{-i},*}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\bigg(2\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})\bigg).

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\|v^{i}_{*}-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},*}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\bigg(2\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})\bigg).

Substituting the previous two inequalities into Eq. (
48
) and we finally obtain

\displaystyle\|v^{i}_{*,\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}-v^{i}_{\pi_{t,K}^{i},\pi_{t,K}^{-i}}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\bigg(2\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+2\|v^{i}_{t}-v^{i}_{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})\bigg).

A.7.6
Proof of Lemma
A.5

For any
i\in\{1,2\}
, we have by the outer-loop update equation (cf. Line
8
) of Algorithm
2
that

\displaystyle v_{t+1}^{i}(s)=\;

\displaystyle\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))+\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))

Since
\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s))=\mathcal{B}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)=v_{*}^{i}(s)
, we have

\displaystyle|v_{t+1}^{i}(s)-v_{*}^{i}(s)|=\;

\displaystyle|\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s))|

\displaystyle+|\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))|.

(51)

For the first term on the RHS of Eq. (
51
), we have by the contraction property of the minimax Bellman operator that

\displaystyle\left|\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s))\right|=\;

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{B}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)-\mathcal{B}^{i}(v_{*}^{i})(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v^{i}_{t}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}.

For the second term on the RHS of Eq. (
51
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))\right|\leq\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\right|}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))\right|}_{T_{2}}

For the term
T_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left\{\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i}-\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right\}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\left\{\max_{\mu^{i}}(\mu^{i}-\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))^{\top}\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)+\tau\nu(\mu^{i})-\tau\nu(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s))\right\}

\displaystyle+2\tau\log(A_{\max})+\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max})+\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}.

Note that
T_{2}
is exactly the term
\hat{E}_{3}
we analyzed in proving Lemma
A.4
. Therefore, we have from Eq. (
50
) that

\displaystyle T_{2}\leq 2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\max_{s}V_{v_{t},s}(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+2\tau\log(A_{\max}).

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\textit{val}^{i}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\max_{s}V(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+\max_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+4\tau\log(A_{\max}).

Using the upper bounds we obtained for the two terms on the RHS of Eq. (
51
) and we have

\displaystyle|v_{t+1}^{i}(s)-v_{*}^{i}(s)|\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}+2\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}V(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

Since the RHS of the previous inequality does not depend on
s
, we have for any
i\in\{1,2\}
that

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}-v_{*}^{i}\|_{\infty}+2\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}V(\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s),\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))+4\tau\log(A_{\max})

\displaystyle+\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i}_{t})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)-q_{t,K}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}+2\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

A.7.7
Proof of Lemma
A.6

Using the outer-loop update equation (cf. Algorithm
2
Line
8
) and we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1,2}v_{t+1}^{i}(s)\right|=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}q_{t,K}^{i}(s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)^{\top}(q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s))\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{i=1,2}\pi_{t,K}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\max_{(s,a^{i},a^{-i})}\left|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v_{t}^{-i})(s,a^{i},a^{-i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}+\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}.

Since the RHS of the previous inequality does not depend on
s
, we in fact have

\displaystyle\|v_{t+1}^{i}+v_{t+1}^{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\|v_{t}^{i}+v_{t}^{-i}\|_{\infty}+\sum_{i=1,2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|q_{t,K}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v_{t}^{i})(s)\pi_{t,K}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}.

A.7.8
Proof of Lemma
A.7

To begin with, observe that

\displaystyle{\arg\max}_{\hat{\mu}^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})\right\}=\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}).

Therefore, the function
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
can be equivalently written as

\displaystyle V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})=\sum_{i=1,2}\left[(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-(\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}-\tau\nu(\mu^{i})\right],

which will frequently used in our analysis.

1.

It is clear that the function
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
is by definition non-negative. The strong convexity follows from the following two observations.

(a)

The negative entropy
-\nu(\cdot)
is
1
– strongly convex with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}

(
Beck, 2017
, Example 5.27)
.

(b)

The following function of
\mu^{i}

\displaystyle(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))=\max_{\hat{\mu}^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{-i})\right\}

is the maximum of linear functions in
\mu^{i}
, and hence is convex.

Therefore, the function
V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
is
\tau
– strongly convex in
\mu^{i}
with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
uniformly for all
\mu^{-i}
.

2.

The smoothness follows from the following two results.

(a)

Since the Hessian matrix of the negative entropy function
-\nu(\mu^{i})
satisfies

\displaystyle H_{-\nu}(\mu^{i})=\text{diag}\left(\frac{1}{\mu^{i}(a_{1}^{i})},\cdots,\frac{1}{\mu^{i}(a_{n}^{i})}\right)\leq\frac{I_{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}}{\delta_{i}}

for all
\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
satisfying
\min_{a^{i}\in\mathcal{A}^{i}}\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\delta_{i}
, we have from the second order characterization of smoothness that
-\nu(\mu^{i})
is a
\frac{1}{\delta_{i}}
– smooth function on
\{\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\mid\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\delta_{i},\forall\;a^{i}\in\mathcal{A}^{i}\}
with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
.

(b)

Using the optimality condition and we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu^{i}}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu^{i}}\max_{\hat{\mu}^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{-i})\right\}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}).

Therefore, using the formula for the gradient of the softmax function
(
Gao and Pavel, 2017
)
, the Hessian
H(\mu^{i})
of the function
(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))
satisfies

\displaystyle H(\mu^{i})=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}X_{-i}^{\top}(\text{diag}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})^{\top})X_{-i}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}X_{-i}^{\top}\text{diag}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))X_{-i}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}X_{-i}^{\top}X_{-i}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}_{\max}(X_{-i})}{\tau}I_{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}

Using the second order characterization of smoothness and we conclude that the function
(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}))
is
\frac{\sigma^{2}_{\max}(X_{-i})}{\tau}
– smooth with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
.

Combining (i) and (ii) and we conclude that the function
V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
is a
(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}(X_{-i})}{\tau}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}})
– smooth function on
\{\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\mid\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\delta_{i},\forall\;a^{i}\in\mathcal{A}^{i}\}
with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}
uniformly for all
\mu^{-i}
.

3.

We first compute the gradient
\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
using Danskin’s theorem:

\displaystyle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})=-(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})\mu^{-i}-\tau\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})+X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}).

(52)

It follows that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle-(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})\mu^{-i}-\tau\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})+X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle-(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})\mu^{-i}-\tau\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})+X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

(53)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\tau\langle\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-\nabla\nu(\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle+(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}).

where Eq. (
53
) is due to the optimality condition
X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))=0
. To proceed, observe that the concavity of
\nu(\cdot)
and the optimality condition
X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))=0
together imply that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-\nabla\nu(\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})-\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})),\mu^{i}-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle\nabla\nu(\mu^{i}),\mu^{i}-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle-\langle\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})),\mu^{i}-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\nu(\mu^{i})-\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-\langle\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})),\mu^{i}-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\nu(\mu^{i})-\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))+\frac{1}{\tau}\langle X_{i}\mu^{-i},\mu^{i}-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}\left[(\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\mu^{i})-\max_{\hat{\mu}^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})\right\}\right].

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left[(\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\mu^{i})-\max_{\hat{\mu}^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle+(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i})

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left[(\mu^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\mu^{-i})-\max_{\hat{\mu}^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{-i})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle+(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})

Adding up the previous two inequalities and we obtain

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle-V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})+(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i})^{\top}(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}).

(54)

To control the second term on the RHS of Eq. (
54
), observe that

\displaystyle(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i})^{\top}(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}+\|\mu^{i}\|_{2})\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle c_{1}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{1}}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle c_{1}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{1}}(\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}).

(55)

To proceed, note that the function

\displaystyle F_{X_{i}}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}):=\max_{\hat{\mu}^{i}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i}-\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})-\tau\nu(\mu^{i})\right\}

is a
\tau
-strongly convex function of
\mu^{i}
uniformly for all
\mu^{-i}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle F_{X_{i}}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})=\;

\displaystyle F_{X_{i}}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})-F_{X_{i}}(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}),\mu^{-i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle F_{X_{i}}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})-\min_{\mu^{i}}F_{X_{i}}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2},

which is called the quadratic growth property in optimization literature. It follows that

\displaystyle\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\tau}\max_{\hat{\mu}^{i}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{i}-\mu^{i})^{\top}X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{i})-\tau\nu(\mu^{i})\right\}.

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\tau}\max_{\hat{\mu}^{-i}}\left\{(\hat{\mu}^{-i}-\mu^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}\mu^{i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\mu}^{-i})-\tau\nu(\mu^{-i})\right\}.

Adding up the previous two inequalities and we have

\displaystyle\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\tau}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}).

Using the previous inequality in Eq. (
55
) and we have

\displaystyle(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i})^{\top}(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle c_{1}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{1}}(\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle c_{1}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2}{c_{1}\tau}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{16}{\tau}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),

where the last line follows from choosing
c_{1}=16/\tau
. Using the previous inequality in Eq. (
54
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{7}{8}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})+\frac{16}{\tau}\|X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}.

4.

For any
u^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
