, using the explicit expression of the gradient of
V_{X}(\cdot,\cdot)
from Eq. (
52
) and we have

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle-(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})\mu^{-i}-\tau\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})+X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\langle-(X_{i}+X_{-i}^{\top})\mu^{-i}-\tau\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})+X_{-i}^{\top}\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle X_{i}\mu^{-i}+\tau\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\tau\langle\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-\nabla\nu(\mu^{i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle

\displaystyle+(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i})^{\top}X_{-i}(\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\tau\|\nabla\nu(\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i}))-\nabla\nu(\mu^{i})\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2},

where the last inequality follows from the smoothness of
\nu(\cdot)
in Lemma
A.7
(2).
Similarly, we also have for any
u^{-i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
that

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|X_{i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{i})\|_{2}.

Adding up the previous two inequalities and we have

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{1}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\rangle+\langle\nabla_{2}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i}),\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}+\|X_{i}\|_{2}+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\right)\left(\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{i})\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}+\|X_{i}\|_{2}+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\right)\bigg[\bar{c}\left(\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\bar{c}}\left(\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}+\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{i})\|_{2}\right)^{2}\bigg]

(This is true for all
\bar{c}>0
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}+\|X_{i}\|_{2}+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\right)\bigg[\bar{c}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})-\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}+\bar{c}\|\sigma_{\tau}(X_{-i}\mu^{i})-\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\bar{c}}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{-i})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{\bar{c}}\|\sigma_{\tau}(u^{-i})-\sigma_{\tau}(X_{i}\mu^{i})\|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

(This is true because
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
for all
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\tau}{\delta_{i}}+\frac{\tau}{\delta_{-i}}+\|X_{i}\|_{2}+\|X_{-i}\|_{2}\right)\bigg[\frac{2\bar{c}}{\tau}V_{X}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|u^{i}-X_{i}\mu^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|u^{-i}-X_{i}\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}\bigg],

where the last line follows from the quadratic growth property of strongly convex functions and the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax function
(
Gao and Pavel, 2017
)
.

A.7.9
Proof of Lemma
A.8

Since
\min_{i=1,2}\min_{s,a^{i}}\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\geq\ell_{\tau}
(cf. Lemma
A.2
), Lemma
A.7
(2) implies that the function
V_{v,s}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
as a function of
\mu^{i}
is
L_{\tau,i}
– smooth on
\{\mu^{i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{i}|}\mid\min_{a^{i}}\mu^{i}(a^{i})\geq\ell_{\tau}\}
uniformly for all
\mu^{-i}
, where

\displaystyle L_{\tau,i}:=\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s))}{\tau}+\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}.

We next bound
L_{\tau,i}
from above.
Since
\|v^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1/(1-\gamma)
and
\|v^{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1/(1-\gamma)
, we have for any
(s,a^{i},a^{-i})
that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s,a^{-i},a^{i})|\leq\;

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{-i}(s,a^{-i},a^{i})|+\gamma\mathbb{E}[|v^{-i}(S_{1})|\mid S_{0}=s,A_{0}^{i}=a^{i},A_{0}^{-i}=a^{-i}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 1+\frac{\gamma}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma},

which implies

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s))=\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s))\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}^{i}||\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}}{1-\gamma}\leq\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma}.

(56)

As a result, we have by
\tau\leq 1
and
\ell_{\tau}\leq 1
that

\displaystyle L_{\tau,i}=\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s))}{\tau}+\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}\leq\frac{A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}\leq\frac{2A_{\max}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}:=L_{\tau}.

Similarly,
V_{v,s}(\mu^{i},\mu^{-i})
as a function of
\mu^{-i}
is also
L_{\tau}
– smooth on the set
\{\mu^{-i}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}\mid\min_{a^{-i}}\mu^{i}(a^{-i})\geq\ell_{\tau}\}
uniformly for all
\mu^{i}
.

Using the smoothness of
V_{v,s}(\cdot,\cdot)
established above, for any
s\in\mathcal{S}
, we have by the policy update equation (cf. Algorithm
3
Line
3
) that

\displaystyle V_{v,s}(\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{2}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\rangle

\displaystyle+\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{1}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{\tau}\beta_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{L_{\tau}\beta_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\underbrace{\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{2}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\rangle}_{\hat{N}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{1}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\rangle}_{\hat{N}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{2}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))\rangle}_{\hat{N}_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\beta_{k}\langle\nabla_{1}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s)),\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{i}(s))-\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\rangle}_{\hat{N}_{4}}

\displaystyle+2L_{\tau}\beta_{k}^{2}.

(57)

We next bound the terms
\{\hat{N}_{j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 4}
on the RHS of Eq. (
57
) using Lemma
A.7
(3) and (4).

First consider
\hat{N}_{1}+\hat{N}_{2}
. We have by Lemma
A.7
(3) that

\displaystyle\hat{N}_{1}+\hat{N}_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{7\beta_{k}}{8}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\frac{16\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i}(s)^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}.

To proceed, note that for any
\mu^{-i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}^{-i}|}
satisfying
\|\mu^{-i}\|_{2}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i}(s)^{\top})\mu^{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\sum_{a^{i}}\left[\sum_{a^{-i}}\mathbb{E}[v^{i}(S_{1})+v^{-i}(S_{1})\mid S_{0}=s,A_{0}^{i}=a^{i},A_{0}^{-i}=a^{-i}]\mu^{-i}(a^{-i})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}\sum_{a^{i}}\left[\sum_{a^{-i}}\mu^{-i}(a^{-i})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}A_{\max}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)+\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i}(s)^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\gamma^{2}A_{\max}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\hat{N}_{1}+\hat{N}_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{7\beta_{k}}{8}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\frac{16A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

We next consider
\hat{N}_{3}+\hat{N}_{4}
. Since

\displaystyle\max(\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\|_{2},\|\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\|_{2})\leq\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma},

(See Eq. (
56
))

we have by Lemma
A.7
(4) that

\displaystyle\hat{N}_{3}+\hat{N}_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}+\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\right)\bigg[\frac{2\bar{c}}{\tau}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|q_{k}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{\bar{c}\tau^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

for any
\bar{c}>0
.
By choosing
\bar{c}=\frac{\tau}{32}(\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}+\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma})^{-1}
,
we have from the previous inequality that

\displaystyle\hat{N}_{3}+\hat{N}_{4}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{k}}{8}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\frac{64\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}+\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\tau^{3}}\|q_{k}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{64\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\tau}{\ell_{\tau}}+\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\tau^{3}}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{k}}{8}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}.

Finally, using the upper bounds we obtained for the terms
\hat{N}_{1}+\hat{N}_{2}
and
\hat{N}_{3}+\hat{N}_{4}
in Eq. (
57
) and we have

\displaystyle V_{v,s}(\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s),\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}}{4}\right)V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))+\frac{16A_{\max}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\|v^{i}+v^{-i}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

\displaystyle+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4A_{\max}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta_{k}^{2}.

Summing up both sides of the previous inequality for all
s
and then taking expectation, and we have the desired result.

A.7.10
Proof of Lemma
A.9

1.

For any
(q_{1}^{i},q_{2}^{i})
and
(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{i}(q_{1}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})-F^{i}(q_{2}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a^{i})}([F^{i}(q_{1}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})](s,a^{i})-[F^{i}(q_{2}^{i},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})](s,a^{i}))^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a^{i})}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})\}}\left(q_{1}^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})-q_{2}^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}.

2.

For any
(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{i}(\bm{0},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})\|_{2}^{2}=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a^{i})}([F^{i}(\bm{0},s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i},s_{1})](s,a^{i}))^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a^{i})}\mathds{1}_{\{(s,a^{i})=(s_{0},a_{0}^{i})\}}\left(\mathcal{R}^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i})+\gamma v^{i}(s_{1})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where the last line follows from
\|v^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1/(1-\gamma)
and
|\mathcal{R}^{i}(s_{0},a_{0}^{i},a_{0}^{-i})|\leq 1
.

3.

We first write down the explicit expression of
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(\cdot)
. Using the definition of
\mathcal{T}^{i}(\cdot)
and we have

\displaystyle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)=\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)\left(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-q^{i}(s)\right),\;\forall\;s\in\mathcal{S},

where
\Pi_{k}^{i}(s):=\text{diag}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s))
. Since
\min_{0\leq k\leq K-1}\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mu_{k}(s)>0
(cf. Lemma
A.2
and Lemma
4.1
(4)) and
\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)
has strictly positive diagonal entries for all
s
and
k
(cf. Lemma
A.2
),
the equation
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})=0
has a unique solution
\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
, which is explicitly given by

\displaystyle\bar{q}_{k}^{i}(s)=\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s),\;\forall\;s\in\mathcal{S}.

4.

Using the explicit expression of
\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(\cdot)
and we have for any
q_{1}^{i}
and
q_{2}^{i}
that

\displaystyle\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{1}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{2}^{i}),q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\rangle=\;

\displaystyle-\sum_{s,a^{i}}\mu_{k}(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)(q_{1}^{i}(s,a^{i})-q_{2}^{i}(s,a^{i}))^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle-\min_{s,a^{i}}\mu_{k}(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle-\mu_{\tau}\ell_{\tau}\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}

(Lemma
4.1
(4) and Lemma
A.2
)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle-c_{\tau}\|q_{1}^{i}-q_{2}^{i}\|_{2}^{2},

where we recall that
c_{\tau}=\mu_{\tau}\ell_{\tau}
.

A.7.11
Proof of Lemma
A.10

For any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2,2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2,3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2,4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]}_{N_{2,5}}

We next control the terms
\{N_{2,j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 5}
on the RHS of the previous inequality. Before that, the following two lemmas are needed. The proof of Lemma
A.13
follows from that of
(
Srikant and Ying, 2019
, Lemma 3)
and
(
Chen et al., 2021b
, Lemma A.3)
. Lemma
A.14
is the policy-counterpart of Lemma
A.13
.

Lemma A.13

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.14
)
.

Given positive integers
k_{1}\leq k_{2}
satisfying
\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\leq 1/4
, we have for any
k\in\{k_{1},k_{1}+1,\cdots,k_{2}\}
that

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\min(2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1},1/2)\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\min(4\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1},1)\left(\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right).

Lemma A.14

(Proof in Appendix
A.7.15
)
.

Given positive integers
k_{1}\leq k_{2}
satisfying
\beta_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\leq 1/4
, we have for any
s\in\mathcal{S}
and
k\in\{k_{1},k_{1}+1,\cdots,k_{2}\}
that

\displaystyle\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k_{1}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\min(2\beta_{k_{1},k_{2}-1},1/2)\left(\|\pi_{k_{1}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+1\right),

\displaystyle\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k_{1}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\min(4\beta_{k_{1},k_{2}-1},1)\left(\|\pi_{k_{2}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+1\right).

We next bound the terms
\{N_{2,j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 5}
. Let
\mathcal{F}_{k}
be the
\sigma
-algebra generated the sequence of random variables
\{S_{0},A_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{-i},\cdots,S_{k-1},A_{k-1}^{i},A_{k-1}^{-i},S_{k}\}
.

The Term
N_{2,1}
.

Using the tower property of conditional expectations and we have

\displaystyle N_{2,1}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle\mathbb{E}[F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}[\underbrace{\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}}_{N_{2,1,1}}],

where the last line follows from
\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}/(1-\gamma)
and similarly
\|\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}/(1-\gamma)
.
For the term
N_{2,1,1}
, using triangle inequality and we have

\displaystyle\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\!-\!\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]\!-\!\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}.

(58)

To control the first term on the RHS of Eq. (
58
), recall that

\displaystyle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)=\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)\left(\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-q^{i}(s)\right),\;\forall\;s\in\mathcal{S},\;q^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|},

where
\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)=\text{diag}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s))
.
Therefore, we have for any
s\in\mathcal{S}
and
q^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
that

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s))q^{i}(s)\|_{2}.

(59)

We next bound the two terms on the RHS of Eq. (
59
). For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\|(\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s))q^{i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\|\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}

(
\|q^{i}(s)\|_{2}\leq\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}
)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\|\mu_{k}(s)(\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s))\|_{2}+\|(\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s))\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\mu_{k}(s)\|\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|\right)

(
\|\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\leq 1
)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\mu_{k}(s)\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}+|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|\right).

For the second term on the RHS of Eq. (
59
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|(\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s))\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\|\mu_{k}(s)\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\mu_{k}(s)\|\Pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\Pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)}{1-\gamma}\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|+(\mu_{k}(s)+\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s))\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}\right).

Using the previous two inequalities in Eq. (
59
) and we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\left(2|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|+(\mu_{k}(s)+\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s))\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}\mu_{k}(s)\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{3A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(4|\mu_{k}(s)-\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)|^{2}+2(\mu_{k}(s)^{2}+\mu_{k-z_{k}}(s)^{2})\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{3A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\mu_{k}(s)^{2}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})\|_{2}^{2}=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})(s)-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{3A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(4\|\mu_{k}-\mu_{k-z_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}+4\max_{s}\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{3A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\max_{s}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}.

Since

\displaystyle\|\mu_{k}-\mu_{k-z_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle|\mathcal{S}|\|\mu_{k}-\mu_{k-z_{k}}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle|\mathcal{S}|\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\|\pi_{k}^{i}-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{\infty}^{2}

(Lemma
4.1
(3))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle|\mathcal{S}|\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle|\mathcal{S}|^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 16|\mathcal{S}|^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}(\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+1)^{2}

(Lemma
A.14
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 64|\mathcal{S}|^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\max_{s}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{s}\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 16\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}\max_{s}(\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+1)^{2}

(Lemma
A.14
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 64\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2},\quad i\in\{1,2\},

we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})\|_{2}^{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{3A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(256|\mathcal{S}|^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}+320\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1728A_{\max}|\mathcal{S}|^{2}\hat{L}_{\tau}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q^{i})\|_{2}\leq\frac{42|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{1/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}

(60)

for all
q^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}^{i}|}
.

We next move on to bound the second term on the RHS of Eq. (
58
). Recall that we denote
P_{\pi}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}|\times|\mathcal{S}|}
as the transition probability matrix of the Markov chain
\{S_{k}\}
induced by the joint policy
\pi
.
Using the definition of conditional expectation and we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{s}\left[\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}\right)(S_{k-z_{k}},s)-\mu_{k}(s)\right]\sum_{a^{i}}\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\sum_{a^{-i}}\pi_{k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{s^{\prime}}p(s^{\prime}|s,a^{i},a^{-i})F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},s,a^{i},a^{-i},s^{\prime})\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{s}\left[\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}\right)(S_{k-z_{k}},s)-\mu_{k}(s)\right]\right|\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\left|\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}\right)(S_{k-z_{k}},s)-\mu_{k}(s)\right|\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{s}\left|\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}\right)(S_{k-z_{k}},s)-P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}(S_{k-z_{k}},s)\right|+\sum_{s}\left|P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}(S_{k-z_{k}},s)-\mu_{k}(s)\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\{\left\|\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}-P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\rho_{\tau}^{z_{k}}\right\}\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

(61)

where the last line follows from Lemma
4.1
(2) and Lemma
A.2
. Observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}-P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}\right\|_{\infty}=\;

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k-\ell+1+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}P_{\pi_{k}}^{\ell-1}-\prod_{j=k+1}^{k-\ell+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}P_{\pi_{k}}^{\ell}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\left(\prod_{j=k+1}^{k-\ell+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}(P_{\pi_{k-\ell+1}}-P_{\pi_{k}})P_{\pi_{k}}^{\ell-1}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\left\|\prod_{j=k+1}^{k-\ell+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}\right\|_{\infty}\|P_{\pi_{k-\ell+1}}-P_{\pi_{k}}\|_{\infty}\|P_{\pi_{k}}^{\ell-1}\|_{\infty}.

Since the induced
\ell_{\infty}
-norm for any stochastic matrix is
1
and
P_{\pi}
as a function of
\pi
is
1
-Lipschitz continuous with respect to the
\ell_{\infty}
-norm, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}-P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\|\pi_{k-\ell+1}-\pi_{k}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{a^{i},a^{-i}}|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{a^{i},a^{-i}}\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(a^{i}|s)|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)|

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{a^{i},a^{-i}}|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(a^{i}|s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)|\pi_{k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(\sum_{a^{-i}}|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)|+\sum_{a^{i}}|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(a^{i}|s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(a^{i}|s)|\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(\|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{1}+\|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle A_{\max}^{1/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{z_{k}}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(\|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}+\|\pi_{k-\ell+1}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 4z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}A_{\max}^{1/2}\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}+\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+2\right)

(Lemma
A.14
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 16z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}A_{\max}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 16z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}A_{\max}^{1/2}.

It then follows from the previous inequality that

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k-z_{k}}]-\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\{\left\|\prod_{j=k+1}^{k+z_{k}}P_{\pi_{j-z_{k}}}-P_{\pi_{k}}^{z_{k}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\rho_{\tau}^{z_{k}}\right\}\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(16A_{\max}^{1/2}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}+2\rho_{\tau}^{z_{k}}\right)\left(\|q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{1-\gamma}\left(16A_{\max}^{1/2}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}+2\rho_{\tau}^{z_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{1-\gamma}\left(16A_{\max}^{1/2}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}+2\beta_{k}\right)

(Definition of
z_{k}
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{36\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|}A_{\max}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}

Substituting the previous inequality and the bound in Eq. (
60
) into Eq. (
58
) and we have

\displaystyle N_{2,1,1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{42|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{1/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{36\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|}A_{\max}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{80|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\hat{L}_{\tau}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}.

It follows that

\displaystyle N_{2,1}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}[N_{2,1,1}]\leq\frac{160|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

The Term
N_{2,2}
.

For any
k\geq z_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,2}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\underbrace{\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}}_{N_{2,2,1}}\underbrace{\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}}_{N_{2,2,2}}]

Using Lemma
A.9
and we have

\displaystyle N_{2,2,1}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\!-\!F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\!+\!F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}

(Lemma
A.1
and Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{2|\mathcal{S}|^{1/2}A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2}{1-\gamma}

(Lemma
A.1
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|^{1/2}A_{\max}^{1/2}}{1-\gamma}.

Moreover, we have by Lemma
A.13
and Lemma
A.1
that

\displaystyle N_{2,2,2}\leq 4\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|^{1/2}A_{\max}^{1/2}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,2}\leq\mathbb{E}[N_{2,2,1}\times N_{2,2,2}]\leq\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

The Term
N_{2,3}
.

For any
k\geq z_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,3}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\underbrace{\|F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}^{2}}_{N_{2,3,1}}]+\frac{1}{2c^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[\underbrace{\|\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}}_{N_{2,3,2}}],

where
c^{\prime}>0
is any positive real number. Since
N_{2,3,1}=N_{2,2,1}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,3,1}\leq\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

To control the term
N_{2,3,2}
, using the explicit expression of
\bar{q}_{k}^{i}
provided in Lemma
A.9
(3) and we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{q}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}.

Since

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k-z_{k}}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4A_{\max}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}(\|\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}+1)

(Lemma
A.14
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{8A_{\max}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma},

we have

\displaystyle N_{2,3,2}\leq\frac{64|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

It follows that

\displaystyle N_{2,3}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{c^{\prime}}{2}\mathbb{E}[N_{2,3,1}]+\frac{1}{2c^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[N_{2,3,2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{8c^{\prime}|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}}{c^{\prime}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{8|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(c^{\prime}+\frac{4A_{\max}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}^{2}}{c^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{3/2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where the last line follows by choosing
c^{\prime}=2A_{\max}^{1/2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}
.

The Term
N_{2,4}
.

For any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,4}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-F^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

(Lemma
A.9
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 4\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\right]

)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where the last line follows from
\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.

The Term
N_{2,5}
.

For any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle N_{2,5}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}),q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\|\bar{F}_{k}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})\|_{2}+\|\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k}^{i})-\bar{F}_{k-z_{k}}^{i}(q_{k-z_{k}}^{i})\|_{2})\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k-z_{k}}^{i}\|_{2}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]+\frac{42|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{1/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

(Lemma
A.9
and Eq. (
60
))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 4\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\right]

(Lemma
A.13
)

\displaystyle+\frac{42|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{1/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{84|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}\hat{L}_{\tau}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{100|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}\hat{L}_{\tau}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Finally, combining the upper bounds we derived for the terms
\{N_{2,j}\}_{1\leq j\leq 5}
and we have

\displaystyle N_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{5}N_{2,j}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{160|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{32|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}^{3/2}\beta_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{16|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{100|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}\hat{L}_{\tau}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{340|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}.

A.7.12
Proof of Lemma
A.11

1.

For any
k\geq 0
, using Lemma
A.9
and we have

\displaystyle\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\|F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\|F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})

\displaystyle+F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}

(
\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

The result follows by taking expectation on both sides of the previous inequality.

2.

For any
k\geq 0
, we have by Lemma
A.9
that

\displaystyle\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\pi_{k+1}^{-i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}^{2}\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}^{2}\sum_{s}(\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}+\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\beta_{k}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

The result follows by taking expectation on both sides of the previous inequality.

3.

For any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\langle q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle\leq\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\|\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where the last inequality follows from Part (1) and Part (2) of this lemma.
The result follows by taking expectation on both sides of the previous inequality.

4.

For any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\sum_{s}\langle q_{k}^{i}(s)-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}(s),\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\hat{c}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}+\frac{\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}}{2\hat{c}}\right),

(62)

where
\hat{c}
is an arbitrary positive real number.
We next analyze the second term on the RHS of the previous inequality. For any
s\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\sigma_{\tau}(\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}(s))+\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\sigma_{\tau}(\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}(s)))\|_{2}}_{B_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}}_{B_{2}}.

Since the softmax operator
\sigma_{\tau}(\cdot)
is
\frac{1}{\tau}
– Lipschitz continuous with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{2}

(
Gao and Pavel, 2017
, Proposition 4)
, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\sigma_{\tau}(\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{A_{\max}}{\tau(1-\gamma)}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s)-\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}.

We next analyze the term
B_{2}
. Using the quadratic growth property of strongly convex functions and we have

\displaystyle B_{2}=\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)\|_{2}\|\sigma_{\tau}(\mathcal{T}^{-i}(v^{-i})(s)\pi_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}A_{\max}}{\sqrt{\tau}(1-\gamma)}V_{v,s}^{1/2}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)).

Combine the upper bounds we obtained for the terms
B_{1}
and
B_{2}
and we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{s}(B_{1}+B_{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\sum_{s}(B_{1}^{2}+B_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\sum_{s}\left(\frac{A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}(s)\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{2A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{4A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s)).

Coming back to Eq. (
62
), using the previous inequality and we have

\displaystyle\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\hat{c}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}+\frac{\sum_{s}\|\mathcal{T}^{i}(v^{i})(s)(\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{-i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\|_{2}^{2}}{2\hat{c}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\hat{c}\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}+\frac{A_{\max}^{2}}{\hat{c}\tau^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\|q_{k}^{-i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{2A_{\max}^{2}}{\hat{c}\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s}V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))\right).

Choosing
\hat{c}=\frac{32A_{\max}^{2}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}
in the previous inequality and then taking total expectation, and we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i},\bar{q}_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\rangle]\leq\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))].

A.7.13
Proof of Lemma
A.12

For
i\in\{1,2\}
, we have from Eq. (
31
), Eq. (
32
), Lemma
A.10
, and Lemma
A.11
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]-\alpha_{k}c_{\tau}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{4|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}(\alpha_{k}^{2}+\alpha_{k}\beta_{k}+\beta_{k}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{340|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\alpha_{k}c_{\tau}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{12|\mathcal{S}|A_{\max}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{340|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\alpha_{k}c_{\tau}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\bar{q}_{k}^{i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\frac{352|\mathcal{S}|^{3/2}A_{\max}^{3/2}\hat{L}_{\tau}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}z_{k}\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k-z_{k},k-1}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{16}\sum_{s}\mathbb{E}[V_{v,s}(\pi_{k}^{i}(s),\pi_{k}^{-i}(s))],

where the second inequality follows from
\beta_{k}=c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}
with
c_{\alpha,\beta}\leq 1
.

A.7.14
Proof of Lemma
A.13

For any
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}-1]
, we have

\displaystyle\|q_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}-\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\|F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\|F^{i}(q_{k}^{i},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})-F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{k}\|F^{i}(\bm{0},S_{k},A_{k}^{i},A_{k}^{-i},S_{k+1})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\alpha_{k}\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

(63)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
A.9
.
Adding
1/(1-\gamma)
to both sides of the previous inequality and we have

\displaystyle\|q_{k+1}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\leq(1+\alpha_{k})\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right).

Repeatedly using the previous inequality and we have for all
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}]
:

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\prod_{j=k_{1}}^{k-1}(1+\alpha_{j})\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}.

Since
1+x\leq e^{x}\leq 1+2x
for all
x\in[0,1/2]
and
\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\leq 1/4
, we have

\displaystyle\prod_{j=k_{1}}^{k-1}(1+\alpha_{j})\leq\exp\left(\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}\right)\leq 1+2\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}.

As a result, we have for all
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}]
that

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}\leq(1+2\alpha_{k_{1},k-1})\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{2\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}}{1-\gamma}.

Using the previous inequality in Eq. (
63
) and we have for any
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}-1]
:

\displaystyle\|q_{k+1}^{i}-q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}\left(\|q_{k}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{k}(1+2\alpha_{k_{1},k-1})\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{2\alpha_{k}\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

where the last line follows from
\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}\leq 1/4
.
Therefore, we have for any
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}]
:

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{j=k_{1}}^{k-1}\|q_{j+1}^{i}-q_{j}^{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\sum_{j=k_{1}}^{k-1}\alpha_{j}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

where the last line follows from
\alpha_{k_{1},k-1}\leq\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}
. This proves the first claimed inequality.

To prove the second claimed inequality, note that

\displaystyle\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}-q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

we have
\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}\leq 4\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}(\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+1/(1-\gamma))
. Therefore, we have for any
k\in[k_{1},k_{2}]
:

\displaystyle\|q_{k}^{i}-q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{1}}^{i}-q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(4\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)+\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\left(\|q_{k_{2}}^{i}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right),

where the last inequality follows from
\alpha_{k_{1},k_{2}-1}\leq 1/4
. The proof is now complete.

A.7.15
Proof of Lemma
A.14

For any
k\geq 0
and
s\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s)\|_{2}-\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\pi_{k+1}^{i}(s)-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}\|\sigma_{\tau}(q_{k}^{i}(s))-\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\beta_{k}(\|\pi_{k}^{i}(s)\|_{2}+1).

The rest of the proof is identical to that of Lemma
A.13
(after Eq. (
63
)).

Appendix B
Proof of Theorem
2.1

Note that Algorithm
1
is a special case of Algorithm
3
when the Markov game has only one state, and the inputs
v^{i}
and
v^{-i}
satisfy
v^{i}=v^{-i}=\bm{0}
. Therefore, Lemma
A.8
and Lemma
A.12
are both applicable, and are restated in the following.

Lemma B.1
.

The following inequality holds for all
k\geq 0
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k+1}^{i},\pi_{k+1}^{-i})]\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{3\beta_{k}}{4}\right)\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})]+\frac{4A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}}

\displaystyle+\frac{256A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}}\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}].

(64)

Lemma B.2
.

The following inequality holds for all
k\geq 0
:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k+1}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\ell_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{17A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\tau}\right)\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+352A_{\max}^{3/2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{\beta_{k}}{8}\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})].

(65)

Adding up Eqs. (
B.2
) and (
B.1
) and we have for any
k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k+1}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k+1}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k+1}^{i},\pi_{k+1}^{-i})]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\left(1-\ell_{\tau}\alpha_{k}+\frac{280A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}}{\ell_{\tau}^{2}\tau^{3}}\right)\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle+\left(1-\frac{\beta_{k}}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})]+\frac{4A_{\max}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}}+352A_{\max}^{3/2}\alpha_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)(\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})])

\displaystyle+\frac{4A_{\max}^{2}c_{\alpha,\beta}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\ell_{\tau}}+356A_{\max}^{3/2}\alpha_{k}^{2},

where the last line follows from Condition
A.1
.
Denote
M_{k}=\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{k}^{i},\pi_{k}^{-i})]+\sum_{i=1,2}\mathbb{E}[\|q_{k}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{k}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}]
. The previous inequality reads

\displaystyle M_{k+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{k}}{2}\right)M_{k}+356A_{\max}^{3/2}\alpha_{k}^{2}.

Repeatedly using the previous inequality and we have for all
k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle M_{k}\leq\prod_{m=0}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)M_{0}+356A_{\max}^{3/2}\sum_{n=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{n}^{2}\prod_{m=n+1}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right).

(66)

Due to our initialization, we have

\displaystyle M_{0}=V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{0}^{i},\pi_{0}^{-i})+\sum_{i=1,2}\|q_{0}^{i}-\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{0}^{-i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 3.

It remains to bound
\prod_{m=0}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)
and
\sum_{n=0}^{k-1}\alpha_{n}^{2}\prod_{m=n+1}^{k-1}\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha_{m}}{2}\right)
when
\alpha_{k}
is explicitly chosen. Using results in
(
Chen et al., 2021b
, Appendix A.2)
and we have the following inequalities:

1.

When using constant stepsize, i.e.,
\alpha_{k}\equiv\alpha
, we have for all
k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle M_{k}\leq 3\left(1-\frac{c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha}{2}\right)^{k}+\frac{712A_{\max}^{3/2}\alpha}{c_{\alpha,\beta}}.

2.

When
\alpha_{k}=\frac{\alpha}{k+h}
with
\alpha c_{\alpha,\beta}=4
and
h
chosen such that
\alpha/h<1
, we have for all
k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle M_{k}\leq 3\left(\frac{h}{k+h}\right)^{2}+356A_{\max}^{3/2}\frac{16e}{c_{\alpha,\beta}}\frac{\alpha}{k+h}.

3.

When
\alpha_{k}=\frac{\alpha}{(k+h)^{z}}
, where
\alpha>0
,
z\in(0,1)
, and
h\geq[4z/(c_{\alpha,\beta}\alpha)]^{1/(1-z)}
, we have for all
k\geq 0
that

\displaystyle M_{k}\leq 3\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha}{2c_{\alpha,\beta}(1-z)}((k+h)^{1-z}-h^{1-z})\right)+356A_{\max}^{3/2}\frac{4}{c_{\alpha,\beta}}\frac{\alpha}{(k+h)^{z}}.

The result follows by observing that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\text{NG}(\pi_{K}^{i},\pi_{K}^{-i})]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\hat{\pi}^{i}}(\hat{\pi}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}-(\pi_{K}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\hat{\pi}^{i}}\left\{(\hat{\pi}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\pi}^{i})\right\}-(\pi_{K}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1,2}\left(\max_{\hat{\pi}^{i}}\left\{(\hat{\pi}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}+\tau\nu(\hat{\pi}^{i})\right\}-(\pi_{K}^{i})^{\top}\mathcal{R}^{i}\pi_{K}^{-i}-\tau\nu(\pi_{K}^{i})\right)\right]+\tau\log(A_{\max}^{2})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[V_{\mathcal{R}}(\pi_{K}^{i},\pi_{K}^{-i})]+\tau\log(A_{\max}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle M_{k}+2\tau\log(A_{\max}).

Appendix C
Proof of Corollary
2.1.2
and Corollary
3.2.2

We first consider Corollary
2.1.2
. The following proof idea was previous used in
Sayin et al., 2021
to show the rationality of their decentralized
Q
-learning algorithm.

Observe that Theorem
2.1
can be easily generalized to the case where the reward is corrupted by noise. Specifically, suppose that player
i
takes action
a^{i}
and player
-i
takes action
a^{-i}
. Instead of assuming player
i
receives a deterministic reward
\mathcal{R}^{i}(a^{i},a^{-i})
, we assume that player
i
receives a random reward
r^{i}(a^{i},a^{-i},\xi)
, where
\xi\in\Xi
(where
\Xi
is a finite set) is a random variable with distribution
\mu_{\xi}
, and is independent of everything else. The proof is identical as long as
r^{i}+r^{-i}=0
, and the reward is uniformly bounded, i.e.,
\max_{a^{i},a^{-i},\xi}|r^{i}(a^{i},a^{-i},\xi)|<\infty
.

Now consider the case where player
i
’s opponent follows a stationary policy
\pi^{-i}
. We incorporate the randomness of player
-i
’s action into the model and introduce a fictitious opponent with only one action
a^{*}
. In particular, let
\hat{r}^{i}(a^{i},a^{*},a^{-i})=\mathcal{R}^{i}(a^{i},a^{-i})
for all
a^{i}
and
a^{-i}
, and let
\hat{p}(s^{\prime}\mid s,a^{i},a^{*})=\sum_{\pi^{-i}(a^{-i}|s)}p(s^{\prime}\mid a^{i},a^{-i},s)
. Now the problem can be reformulated as player
i
playing against the fictitious player with a single action
a^{*}
, with reward function
\hat{r}^{i}
(
i\in\{1,2\}
) and transition probabilities
\hat{p}
. Applying Theorem
2.1
and we have the
\mathcal{O}(1/\epsilon)
sample complexity for player
i
to find its best response against
\pi^{-i}
, up to a smoothing bias.

The proof of Corollary
3.2.2
is identical to that of Corollary
2.1.2
.

Appendix D
On the Mixing Time of MDPs with Almost Deterministic Policies

Consider an MDP with two states
s_{1},s_{2}
and two actions
a_{1},a_{2}
. The transition probability matrix
P_{1}
of taking action
a_{1}
is the identity matrix
I_{2}
, and the transition probability matrix
P_{2}
of taking action
a_{2}
is
P_{2}=[0,1;1,0]
. Given
\alpha\in(1/2,1)
, let
\pi_{\alpha}
be a policy such that
\pi(a_{1}|s)=\alpha
and
\pi(a_{2}|s)=1-\alpha
for any
s\in\{s_{1},s_{2}\}
. Denote
P_{\alpha}
as the transition probability matrix under
\pi_{\alpha}
. It is easy to see that

\displaystyle P_{\alpha}=\begin{bmatrix}\alpha&1-\alpha\\
1-\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}.

Since
P_{\alpha}
is a doubly stochastic matrix, and has strictly positive entries, it has a unique stationary distribution
\mu=\bm{1}^{\top}/2
.

We next compute a lower bound of the mixing time of the
\pi_{\alpha}
-induced Markov chain.
Let
e_{1}=[1,0]^{\top}
be the initial distribution of the states, and denote
[x_{k},1-x_{k}]^{\top}
as the distribution of the states at time step
k
. Then we have

\displaystyle x_{k+1}=\;

\displaystyle x_{k}\alpha+(1-x_{k})(1-\alpha)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(2\alpha-1)x_{k}+1-\alpha

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(2\alpha-1)^{k+1}x_{0}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}(1-\alpha)(2\alpha-1)^{k-i}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}+\frac{(2\alpha-1)^{k+1}}{2}.

It follows that

\displaystyle t_{\pi_{\alpha},\eta}=\;

\displaystyle\min_{k\geq 0}\left\{\max_{\mu_{0}\in\Delta^{2}}\left\|\mu_{0}^{\top}P_{\alpha}^{k}-\bm{1}^{\top}/2\right\|_{\text{TV}}\leq\eta\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\min_{k\geq 0}\left\{\left\|e_{1}^{\top}P_{\alpha}^{k}-\bm{1}^{\top}/2\right\|_{\text{TV}}\leq\eta\right\}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\min_{k\geq 0}\left\{(2\alpha-1)^{k}\leq 2\eta\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\log(1/2\eta)}{\log(1/(2\alpha-1))}-1,

which implies
\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 1}t_{\alpha,\eta}=\infty
. Therefore, as the policies become deterministic, the mixing time of the associated Markov chain can approach infinity.
